DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_0 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_0/ADDRARDADDR[10] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/frame_count[5]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/frame_count_reg[5]) that has an active asychronous set or reset. DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_0 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_0/WEA[1] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/error_reg) that has an active asychronous set or reset. DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_1 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_1/ADDRBWRADDR[12] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_0_0[9]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/cfg_reg[14][9]) that has an active asychronous set or reset. This may cause corruption of the memory contents and/or read values when the set/reset is asserted and is not analyzed by the default static timing analysis. It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_1 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_1/WEA[2] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/map_fault_latched_reg) that has an active asychronous set or reset. DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_2 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_2/WEA[0] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/bank/command_error_reg) that has an active asychronous set or reset. This may cause corruption of the memory contents and/or read values when the set/reset is asserted and is not analyzed by the default static timing analysis. It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

CLASS         string  true       drc_violation
DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_2 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_2/WEA[1] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/reset_sync_reg[1]) that has an active asychronous set or reset. This may cause corruption of the memory contents and/or read values when the set/reset is asserted and is not analyzed by the default static timing analysis. It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

NAME          string  true       REQP-1839#246
OBJECT_TYPES  string  true       cell net pin
SEVERITY      string  true       Warning
WAIVER        string  true       
Property      Type    Read-only  Value
CHECK         string  true       REQP-1839
CLASS         string  true       drc_violation
DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_2 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_2/WEA[3] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/c16.adc/error_reg) that has an active asychronous set or reset. This may cause corruption of the memory contents and/or read values when the set/reset is asserted and is not analyzed by the default static timing analysis. It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_3 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_3/ADDRBWRADDR[11] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_0_0[8]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/cfg_reg[14][8]) that has an active asychronous set or reset. It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.

DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_3 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_3/WEA[2] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/enable_sync_reg[1]) that has an active asychronous set or reset. Property      Type    Read-only  Value
CHECK         string  true       REQP-1839
CLASS         string  true       drc_violation
DESCRIPTION   string  true       The RAMB36E1 implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_3 has an input control pin implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/memory_reg_3/WEA[3] (net: implementation/core/native_system/digital/scheduler/WEA[0]) which is driven by a register (implementation/core/native_system/digital/serial/overrun_reg) that has an active asychronous set or reset. This may cause corruption of the memory contents and/or read values when the set/reset is asserted and is not analyzed by the default static timing analysis. It is suggested to eliminate the use of a set/reset to registers driving this RAMB pin or else use a synchronous reset in which the assertion of the reset is timed by default.
